### [112] [From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yuanxi Che,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"结晶问题"（crystallization problem），即如何度量测试时计算（如文本到SQL查询修复）的返回值。通过受控评估，在固定单次求解器的情况下逐一改变记忆选择，分别测量重放、跨问题保留和同库留出转移。在BIRD基准上，存储已验证的修正查询使留出首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，捕获了按需修复在相同问题上44.4%的准确率余量。实验表明数据库特定内容是主要运作成分，可靠验证和更广的检索覆盖带来稳定增益，而更丰富的格式和复杂的检索器并非如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放可以纠正困难的文本到SQL查询，但额外计算通常在每次回答后被丢弃。系统日益保留已验证的修复片段，然而评估仍然只报告单一端到端分数，无法区分重放对重复问题的帮助与对未见问题的帮助，也无法识别负责的记忆选择。

Method: 采用受控评估方法，固定单次求解器不变，一次仅改变一个记忆选择。分别测量三个维度：重放（replay）、跨问题保留（cross-question retention）以及同数据库留出转移（held-out same-database transfer）。

Result: 在BIRD基准上，存储已验证的修正查询使留出首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，该增益捕获了按需修复在相同问题上44.4%的准确率余量。受控干预识别出数据库特定内容为主要运作成分。可靠验证和更广的检索覆盖产生可支持的增益；更丰富的格式和精心设计的检索器并未奏效。

Conclusion: 测试时计算通过记忆结晶可以惠及未见问题，而不仅仅是重复问题。数据库特定内容是记忆发挥作用的关键，简单可靠的验证与检索覆盖比复杂格式和检索器更重要。代码、评估工件和复现说明已开源。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can correct difficult text-to-SQL queries, but the extra computation is normally discarded after each answer. Systems increasingly retain verified repair episodes, yet evaluations still report one end-to-end score. It cannot distinguish replay on recurring questions from help on unseen questions, or identify the responsible memory choice. We call measuring this future value the crystallization problem. Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one memory choice at a time. We separately measure replay, cross-question retention, and held-out same-database transfer. On BIRD, storing verified corrected queries improves held-out first-attempt accuracy by 4.34 percentage points. This gain captures 44.4% of the accuracy headroom provided by on-demand repair on the same questions. Controlled interventions identify database-specific content as the main operating ingredient. Reliable verification and broader retrieval coverage yield supported gains; richer formats and elaborate retrievers do not. Open-source code, evaluation artifacts, and reproduction instructions are available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization.

</details>


### [113] [Skaling: Chinchilla's Exponents Meet Kaplan's Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07222)
*Mathurin Videau,Badr Youbi-Idrissi,David Lopez-Paz,Kartik Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Skaling law，一种将模型容量与数据通过单一交互指数耦合的广义神经缩放定律，相较于标准公式在数据稀缺和过度训练极端场景下将MAPE降低1.5-3倍，并结合稀疏网格策略可在节省约10倍计算量的情况下实现精确的全网格外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经缩放定律假设模型大小和训练数据对损失的影响是独立的，导致在数据稀缺和过度训练等极端情况下系统性地低估或高估损失，无法准确预测模型性能。

Method: 提出Skaling law，一种广义函数形式，通过单一交互指数将模型容量（model capacity）和训练数据（data）耦合在一起，打破两者独立的假设。同时引入稀疏网格策略，仅在低计算量区域采样，实现全网格外推预测。

Result: 在插值和外推两种场景下，Skaling law将MAPE降低1.5-3倍；结合稀疏网格策略，仅需约1/10的计算量即可达到均匀扫描的准确全网格外推效果。

Conclusion: Skaling law通过耦合模型容量与数据规模，提供了一种更稳健且资源高效的性能预测框架，能够从小规模实验中可靠预测模型性能，为下一代模型训练的计算预算分配提供指导。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws are foundational for language model development, yet standard formulations systematically under- and overestimate loss at data-scarce and overtraining extremes. This failure originates in the underlying assumption that model size and training data impact the loss independently. To address this, we introduce the Skaling law, a generalized functional form that couples model capacity and data through a single interaction exponent. This simple extension reduces the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) by 1.5-3x across both interpolation and extrapolation regimes. When paired with a sparse grid strategy restricted to low-compute regimes, the Skaling law achieves accurate full-grid extrapolation using approximately 10x less compute than uniform sweeps. By enabling reliable performance prediction from small-scale experiments, the Skaling law provides a more robust and resource-efficient framework for allocating compute budgets in next-generation model training.

</details>


### [114] [Stoicheia: Character-Level Masked Diffusion for Ancient Greek Textual Restoration, Parsing, and Metrical Scansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07249)
*Eric Cullhed,Albin Thörn Cleland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Stoicheia，一个405M参数的基于字符级掩码扩散编码器，用于古希腊语文本。输入分解为五个可独立掩码的平面（字母、词/句边界、变音符、大小写、标点），单个骨干网络即可统一处理补缺、重分段、加重音和加标点任务。基于380M词开源语料预训练，发布11个检查点（10个旋转去污折叠+1个无文档文本暴露）。三个实验（碑文/纸草修复、句法标注与依存解析、音量符号与格律分析）均配随机初始化对照，验证预训练贡献：碑文重建CER提升5.6点、解析LAS提升12.9、音量符号平衡准确率提升6.0点。在Ithaca测试集上，CER从24.6（Ithaca）和23.5（Aeneas）降至15.5，top-1准确率从63.0/64.0提升至74.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语文献研究中存在多种相互关联的文本处理需求（补缺、重分段、加重音、加标点），传统方法需要针对每个任务进行专门的重新分词和模型训练，效率低且无法共享底层表征。研究者希望构建一个统一的、字符级的多任务模型，既能处理古典文本的各种形态学任务，又能确保评估时避免数据污染（通过旋转去污折叠保证每个文学段落至少有一个模型从未见过）。

Method: 采用405M参数的字符级掩码扩散编码器。核心创新在于输入分解为五个对齐且可独立掩码的平面：字母平面、词/句边界平面、变音符平面、大小写平面、标点平面。这种多平面分解使得单一骨干网络可以在推理时通过掩码不同平面来执行不同任务，无需任务特定重分词。预训练语料为380M词的开源、版本固定的语料库。训练后发布11个检查点（10个旋转去污折叠+1个排除文档文本的检查点）。

Result: 三个实验均配有随机初始化对照以隔离预训练贡献：碑文/纸草重建CER改善5.6点，依存解析LAS提升12.9点，音量符号与格律分析的平衡准确率提升6.0点。在Ithaca测试集上（相同冻结样本、严格评分），CER从Ithaca的24.6和Aeneas框架后继版的23.5降至15.5；top-1准确率从63.0（Ithaca）和64.0（Aeneas）提升至74.5。

Conclusion: Stoicheia证明字符级掩码扩散预训练在古希腊语任务上具有显著优势：统一的多平面架构能在不重分词的情况下处理多种文本修复与标注任务；旋转去污策略保障了评估的可信度；与专有任务模型（Ithaca）相比在碑文重建上取得大幅性能提升。该工作为古典文献的计算处理提供了一个开源、可复现且任务统一的强基线。

Abstract: We introduce Stoicheia, a 405M-parameter character-level masked-diffusion encoder for Ancient Greek whose input factors into five aligned, independently maskable planes: letters, word and sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, and punctuation. A single backbone can therefore restore lacunae, re-segment, accentuate, and punctuate unspaced text without task-specific retokenization. We pretrain it on an open, revision-pinned corpus of 380M words and release eleven checkpoints: ten rotated, decontaminated folds, guaranteeing that for any given literary passage at least one released model has never seen its text, and one with no exposure to documentary texts. Three experiments - reconstruction of damaged inscriptions and papyri, morphosyntactic tagging and dependency parsing, and macronization with metrical scansion - each carry a matched random-initialization control, isolating what character-level diffusion pretraining contributes: 5.6 CER points on inscription reconstruction, 12.9 LAS on parsing, and 6.0 points of balanced accuracy on macronization. On Ithaca's own test split, with identical frozen samples and strict scoring, Stoicheia reduces character error relative to both prior state-of-the-art systems, from 24.6 (Ithaca) and 23.5 (its 2025 Aeneas-framework successor) to 15.5, and raises top-1 accuracy from 63.0 and 64.0 to 74.5.

</details>


### [115] [Why Knowing Both Hops Is Not Enough: Understanding Two-Hop Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07261)
*Zili Zhang,Yilin Wang,Heng Wang,Herun Wan,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在受控符号环境中从头训练Transformer，研究大语言模型在简单两跳查询上失败的内部机制。研究发现：当第二跳符合训练分布时模型泛化可靠，偏离时则失败。机制分析表明，成功泛化源于跨上下文一致中间表示的出现，而失败源于上下层不匹配——下层正确构建中间表示，上层却只学会映射而非推理。基于此提出了循环式训练策略，使Transformer跨输入形式复用推理电路，显著提升了分布外两跳查询的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能解决复杂的多跳问题，却会在简单的两跳查询上出现令人困惑的失败：模型可能正确存储每个单独跳的信息，却无法将它们组合起来。理解这一现象的内部机制对于改进模型的推理能力至关重要。

Method: 在受控符号环境中从头训练Transformer，通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis）研究两跳泛化行为，并基于洞察提出循环式（recurrent-style）训练策略。

Result: 发现两跳泛化的规律模式：当第二跳符合训练分布时模型可靠泛化，偏离时总是失败。机制分析完整解释了这两种不同的泛化行为，提出的循环式训练策略显著改善了分布外两跳查询的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 成功泛化依赖于跨上下文一致的中间表示；失败源于上层网络将中间表示直接映射到输出而非进行推理。循环式训练策略通过让模型复用推理电路，有效改善了分布外两跳泛化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve complex multi-hop problems yet exhibit puzzling failures on simple two-hop queries: although a model may correctly store each individual hop, it often fails to combine them. To understand the internal mechanisms of this phenomenon, we train transformers from scratch in a controlled symbolic environment. Our experiments reveal a pattern in two-hop generalization: models generalize reliably when the second hop follows the training distribution, but always fail when it deviates.
  Through mechanistic analysis, we provide a complete explanation for these distinct generalization behaviors: in settings where models generalize successfully, performance is driven by the emergence of consistent intermediate representations for the same entities across contexts, whereas failures on settings where the second hop is out-of-distribution arise from a mismatch across layers: lower layers correctly construct these intermediate representations, but upper layers, while trained on corresponding atomic facts, primarily learn to map them to outputs rather than to reason over them.
  Driven by this insight, we propose a recurrent-style training strategy, which enables transformers to reuse their reasoning circuitry across input forms and substantially improves generalization on out-of-distribution two-hop queries.

</details>


### [116] [Gaze Behavior in Visual World Experiments Can be Modeled With Off-the-shelf Language-Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07282)
*Rahul Murali Shankar,Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的方法来预测视觉世界研究中的注视行为，通过结合CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型与双模态归因方法，无需生成架构和微调即可复现一项经典英语视觉世界研究的结果，展示人类预测性加工的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往计算心理语言学主要关注单模态（书面或口语）的神经语言模型，而忽视了多模态实验范式（如视觉世界研究）中同时呈现视觉和语言输入的情况。

Method: 结合一个简单的CLIP家族多模态双编码器模型与一种双模态归因方法，用于预测视觉世界研究中的注视行为。

Result: 该方法能够稳健地复现一项经典英语视觉世界研究的结果，展示人类预测性加工，且无需生成架构、无需微调，尽管模型并未为这一任务专门训练。

Conclusion: 这表明现成的多模态编码器模型即使不经过生成架构或微调，也能有效预测人类在多模态语言理解任务中的注视行为，为计算心理语言学提供了新的多模态研究方向。

Abstract: The recent advances in neural language models have also spurred much work in computational psycholinguistics, asking whether neural LMs are also promising models of human language processing. However, work has been overwhelmingly focused on the unimodal case of written or spoken language. In contrast, multimodal experimental paradigms, like visual world studies that present participants with both visual and linguistic input simultaneously, have been neglected. In this paper, we present a novel approach that predicts gaze behavior in visual world studies. It does so by combining a simple multi-modal bi-encoder model of the CLIP family with a bimodal attribution method. We demonstrate the ability of this approach to robustly replicate the results of a seminal English visual world study which shows hu- man predictive processing. Remarkably, it does so without a generative architecture and without the need for fine-tuning, despite not being trained for this task.

</details>


### [117] [Grammar Engineering Meets LLMs: Development of Cantonese and Irish ParGram Treebanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07283)
*Chit-Fung Lam,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在ParGram平行语法项目框架下开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库，并探索使用多语言大语言模型（gpt-oss-120b）辅助语法工程的潜力与局限。结果显示LLM在翻译任务上表现不佳且不受提示语言影响，在句法结构生成上虽能产出部分有意义的结构，但在跨语言抽象任务上表现较差，但仍可作为参考建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法工程需要专业的形式语言学知识和计算实现能力，尤其在平行语法项目中需要在跨语言一致性与语言特异性之间取得平衡。作者希望探索LLM能否辅助这一专业性强的工程过程，同时通过开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库来检验其方法论可行性。

Method: 在ParGram框架内构建粤语和爱尔兰语树库，保持抽象功能层面的语言平行性；同时利用OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b模型进行两类实验：(1) 粤语-爱尔兰语互译测试；(2) 形式句法结构生成测试，评估LLM在语法工程中的辅助潜力。

Result: 翻译性能整体不理想，且不受提示语言影响；句法结构生成任务中模型能产出部分结构上有意义的输出，但在需要跨语言抽象的任务上表现较差。LLM输出对谓词-论元关系有部分捕捉能力，可提供替代分析建议作为参考。

Conclusion: LLM在协作式语法工程中既有潜力也有明显局限，专家驱动的人工分析与验证依然不可或缺。LLM生成结果可作为辅助参考，但不能替代领域专家的判断。

Abstract: Grammar engineering requires expertise in linguistic formalism and computational implementation, especially in parallel grammar projects that balance cross-linguistic consistency with language-specific properties. This paper presents the development of Cantonese and Irish treebanks within the Parallel Grammar (ParGram) Project, where linguistic parallelism is maintained at an abstract functional level. We also investigate the methodological potential and limitations of using multilingual LLMs to support grammar engineering, focusing on Cantonese-Irish translation and the generation of formal syntactic structures using OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b model. The results show that translation performance was generally unsatisfactory and unaffected by prompt language. For syntactic structure generation, the model produced some structurally meaningful outputs, but performed poorly on tasks requiring cross-linguistic abstraction. Nonetheless, LLM-generated outputs may still offer some reference value by suggesting alternative analyses and (partially) capturing predicate-argument relations. Overall, our findings highlight both the potential and limitations of using LLMs in collaborative grammar engineering, while underscoring the continued importance of expert-driven analysis and verification.

</details>


### [118] [Natural Language Processing Psychometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07316)
*Edoardo Sebastiano De Duro,Emma Franchino,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"NLP心理测量学"框架，利用9个LLM在受控人格条件（认知数字影子）下完成心理问卷并生成文本解释，提取情绪特征和句法语义网络拓扑特征，结合人格与社会人口学变量进行随机森林回归预测心理健康结果。全模型可解释生活满意度70.8%方差、抑郁55.7%，且无需重新训练即可迁移到真实人类文本，但合成数据不能替代人类验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP心理健康预测模型缺乏明确的测量目标定义（不知测量的是语境知识、情绪内容还是句法结构），且预测缺乏可解释性和可测试性，无法确证模型究竟在测量什么心理构念。

Method: （1）9个LLM在受控人格人设（认知数字影子）下完成心理测量问卷并逐项提供文本解释；（2）从文本中提取情绪档案和句法语义结构（forma mentis网络）特征；（3）结合人格与社会人口学变量，用消融随机森林回归器预测心理健康结果；（4）使用SHAP分析特征贡献方向；（5）在不重新训练的情况下，将模型迁移到人类日记和真实临床转录上验证泛化性。

Result: 全模型解释生活满意度70.8%、抑郁55.7%（PHQ-9）方差；DASS-21上抑郁68.5%、焦虑76.0%、压力72.4%。社会人口学变量对抑郁/焦虑/压力无解释力但对生活满意度有效。神经质和网络拓扑主导抑郁和焦虑预测且方向相反。零训练迁移到人类日记r高达0.91，仅用网络/情绪特征对真实临床转录分类准确率达68%。

Conclusion: LLM人格人设能够暴露模型偏见、恢复与临床思维反刍一致的模式，并支持无需匹配问卷的人类文本心理测量预测，但合成数据不能替代人类验证。NLP心理测量学通过可解释AI和网络/情绪特征使心理测量的目标区分变得明确、可测量、可检验。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) models predicting mental health outcomes rarely specify what they measure: contextual knowledge, emotional content, or syntactic structure. NLP Psychometrics treats psychological prediction from text as a psychometric problem, linking scores to interpretable linguistic evidence and testing beyond the training text format. Nine LLMs, conditioned on controlled personas (cognitive digital shadows), completed psychometric questionnaires with textual explanations per item. We extracted emotional profiles and syntactic-semantic structure via textual forma mentis networks, combined with personality and sociodemographic variables in ablated random forest (RF) regressors, using SHAP to identify which features drove performance and in which direction. Full RF models explained up to 70.8% of variance in life satisfaction (SWLS), 55.7% in depression (PHQ-9), and, for DASS-21, 68.5% depression, 76.0% anxiety, 72.4% stress. Sociodemographics alone explained no meaningful variance in depression, anxiety, or stress, but did so for life satisfaction, where emotion features and income were the strongest predictors; neuroticism and network topology instead dominated depression and anxiety, reversing direction between them. Without retraining, RF models separated diaries from low- and high-score personas ($r$ up to 0.91) and, using only network/emotion features, classified clinical from control participants in real transcripts with up to 68% accuracy. These results show the promise and limits of synthetic data: LLM personas can expose model biases, recover patterns consistent with clinical rumination, and support psychometric prediction from human text without a matched questionnaire, but cannot substitute for human validation. NLP Psychometrics makes these distinctions explicit, measurable, and testable through interpretable AI and network/emotional features.

</details>


### [119] [Zero Gap Is Not Restoration: Stratified Per-Question Probability Evaluation and Step-wise Mitigation of Benchmark Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07341)
*Ruijie Hou,Yueyang Jiao,Zhao Wang,Yingming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-PPG作为更可靠的污染缓解评估指标，并设计RailCap方法在解码时动态抑制记忆化；实验表明SA-PPG揭示先前策略的恢复效果被高估，RailCap获得最低SA-PPG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共基准测试数据不可避免地泄露进预训练语料，导致评估分数虚高。现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP存在缺陷：离散化的正确/错误读取无法刻画每问性能，先平均后差分使过度与不足抑制相互抵消，且均匀权重会促使策略将求解概率推向干净模型的高频值。

Method: 提出SA-PPG指标：通过采样估计每题求解概率，与干净模型逐题差分，并按干净模型求解概率分组聚合。提出RailCap方法：在生成过程中实时判断污染，当样本回落到贪心轨迹时，将下一token钳制为亚军，累积抑制直到响应分布足够分散。

Result: 在多个受污染模型与基准上的实验显示，SA-PPG揭示先前污染缓解策略的恢复效果被大幅高估，而RailCap取得了最低的SA-PPG。

Conclusion: SA-PPG提供了更准确的污染缓解评估框架，RailCap通过解码期动态干预有效抑制记忆化，显著优于依赖污染估计的现有策略。

Abstract: Test data from public benchmarks inevitably leaks into pretraining corpora, inflating evaluation scores once memorized. \textbf{Contamination mitigation evaluation} intervenes in the decoding process to suppress memorization and restore a contaminated model's genuine capability, but its prevailing metric, the \textbf{G-AP} (\textbf{G}ap of \textbf{A}ggregate \textbf{P}erformance), is flawed. Discrete correct/incorrect readouts cannot characterize per-question performance, averaging before differencing lets over- and under-suppression cancel out, and uniform per-question weighting invites strategies to push solve probabilities onto the clean model's high-frequency values. We propose \textbf{SA-PPG} (\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{A}ggregate of \textbf{P}er-question \textbf{P}robability \textbf{G}aps): estimate each question's solve probability by sampling, difference it against the clean model per question, and aggregate within groups defined by the clean model's solve probability. Existing mitigation strategies first estimate where contamination lies and then operate on the estimate, so they are only as correct as the estimate. \textbf{RailCap} instead judges contamination during generation: whenever a sample falls back onto the greedy trajectory, the next trajectory token is capped to the runner-up, accumulating suppression until the response distribution becomes sufficiently dispersed. Across multiple contaminated models and benchmarks, SA-PPG reveals that prior strategies' restoration is substantially overestimated, while RailCap attains the lowest SA-PPG.

</details>


### [120] [Geo-Spatial Concept Probing of Large Language Models: Abstraction, Compositionality, and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07353)
*Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型（LLMs）在概念理解方面的不足，设计了一个以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为核心的概念基准测试，通过问答任务来受控地探测LLMs在抽象性、组合性和接地性三个核心属性上的表现，并系统分析了模型规模和训练方式对概念理解能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽在自然语言基准或合成任务上评估了LLMs的概念理解，但这些设置常混淆多种技能或缺乏对底层概念及其属性的精确控制，难以有针对性地探测模型的概念理解能力。

Method: 设计以概念核心属性（抽象性、组合性、接地性）为目标的受控探测框架；构建以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为中心的概念基准；使用问答任务作为代理评估手段；在多种LLM架构与训练制度下进行大规模实验。

Result: 实验揭示了当前LLMs在概念理解上存在明显的局限性，并识别出影响模型获取和组合结构化概念能力的关键因素（如模型规模和设计）。

Conclusion: 研究为重新设计基于概念的LLMs以改进信息访问和知识管理提供了洞察与方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding concepts is fundamental to generalization. Despite their impressive performance on a wide range of tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with genuine concept understanding. Prior work has evaluated conceptual understanding in LLMs using natural-language benchmarks or narrowly scoped synthetic tasks, but these settings often conflate multiple skills or lack precise control over the underlying concepts and their properties. To support controlled probing of concepts in LLMs, we design tests on their core properties: abstraction, compositionality, and groundness. We set up a concept-centric benchmark, targeting spatial concepts such as direction, distance, topology, and their compositions, and use question answering tasks serving as a proxy. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple LLM architectures and training regimes to analyze how model scale and design impact conceptual understanding. The results reveal clear limitations in current LLMs and provide insights into the factors shaping their ability to acquire and compose structured concepts. Our findings shed light on how concept-based LLMs can be redesigned for improved information access and knowledge management. The code will be available at https://github.com/rd20karim/concept-probing.

</details>


### [121] [LitTraceQA: A Benchmark for Multi-Stage Grounding and Verification in Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07370)
*Xuye Liu,Yimu Wang,Peng Shi,Bo Xue,Xiangrui Ke,Songcheng Cai,Kath Choi,Di Wu,Freda Shi,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LitTraceQA，一个面向科学论文的文献溯源性问答基准。给定研究问题和论文元数据池，系统需返回论文标识符、支持证据位置和答案（自由文本、选择题或结构化表格）。开发集含55个示例，最终标注集含4,978个问题记录和4,859篇论文，分别评估论文检索、证据溯源和答案准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献被广泛用作语言模型、检索增强生成系统和研究助手的知识来源，但仅靠流畅生成无法满足回答研究问题的需求。可靠系统必须能识别相关论文、定位具体证据，并生成忠实于证据的回答。现有工作缺乏同时涵盖这三项能力的基准。

Method: 构建LitTraceQA基准：给定研究问题和论文元数据池，系统需输出三个关联结果——规范化论文标识符、支持证据位置（涵盖表格、图表、文本片段、公式/算法、引文上下文等证据类型），以及符合要求的答案格式（自由文本、多选题、结构化表格）。公开开发集含55个示例（26个隐藏来源单论文问题和29个多论文问题），并提供金标准论文、证据标注和答案。

Result: 开发集包含55个示例，覆盖单论文和多论文问题；最终标注集包含4,978个唯一问题记录和4,859篇唯一金标准论文。基准分别评估论文检索、证据溯源和答案准确性三个维度。

Conclusion: LitTraceQA为科学QA系统提供了一个可验证答案而非无依据摘要的测试平台，通过独立评估检索、证据溯源和答案准确性，推动可溯源、可验证的科学文献问答系统发展。

Abstract: Scientific literature is increasingly used as a knowledge source for language models, retrieval-augmented generation systems, and research assistants, but answering research questions from papers requires more than fluent generation. A reliable system must identify the relevant papers, locate the concrete evidence that supports the answer, and produce a response that is faithful to that evidence. We present LitTraceQA, a benchmark for literature-grounded question answering over scientific papers. Given a research question and a metadata pool of papers, a system must return three connected outputs: canonical paper identifiers, supporting evidence locations, and answers in one or more requested formats, including free-form text, multiple-choice answers, and structured tables. LitTraceQA targets evidence types common in scientific reading: tables, figures, text spans, equations or algorithms, and citation contexts. The public development split contains 55 examples, including 26 hidden-source single-paper questions and 29 multi-paper questions, and provides gold papers, evidence annotations, and answers for local validation. We also analyze a larger final annotation collection with 4,978 unique-question records over 4,859 unique gold papers. By evaluating paper retrieval, evidence grounding, and answer accuracy separately, LitTraceQA provides a testbed for scientific QA systems that produce verifiable answers rather than unsupported summaries.

</details>


### [122] [An Exploratory Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Rewriting of Moderate-Complexity Financial Sentences for DisCoCat-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07439)
*Brian Llinas,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助的文本重写预处理流程，将中等复杂度的金融情感分析句子压缩、简化或分解为更适合DisCoCat量子自然语言处理（QNLP）框架处理的变体，在保持情感含义的同时降低电路复杂度。最强压缩变体使量子比特和门数平均减少超过70%，GPT-4.1-mini配合Prompt B达到0.550±0.035的最高平均准确率，优于基线0.521±0.050，但训练集大小与准确率呈中等负相关（r=-0.446）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的DisCoCat在金融情感分析中存在实际局限性，包括对解析器敏感、模拟成本高、难以处理较长句子。研究者希望通过LLM辅助的受控重写来扩展DisCoCat对中等复杂度句子的适用性，从而推动QNLP在金融领域更可扩展的应用。

Method: 采用LLM辅助的预处理工作流，通过受控重写策略对中等复杂度的金融情感句子进行压缩、简化或分解，使其成为解析器兼容且电路高效的变体。系统比较了不同的提示策略（prompting strategies）、语言模型以及过滤配置，并以Stein等人的低复杂度DisCoCat为基线进行评估。

Result: 最强的压缩变体在电路层面将平均量子比特和门数减少超过70%。GPT-4.1-mini搭配Prompt B在多次训练中达到最高平均准确率0.550±0.035，高于基线的0.521±0.050。训练集大小与准确率呈中等负相关（Pearson r=-0.446），说明更大的训练划分不一定提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 结果提供了探索性证据，表明LLM辅助重写可以使部分中等复杂度输入在评估的DisCoCat配置下可用，同时强调了提示设计、过滤和电路感知预处理对更可扩展的基于QNLP的金融情感分析的重要性。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) provides a grammar-aware framework for text modeling, and Distributional Compositional Categorical (DisCoCat) is one of its theoretically grounded formulations. Prior work on financial sentiment analysis has identified practical limitations of DisCoCat, including parser sensitivity, high simulation cost, and difficulty handling longer sentences. We study an LLM-assisted preprocessing workflow that uses controlled rewriting to compress, simplify, or decompose moderate-complexity financial sentiment sentences into parser-compatible, circuit-efficient variants while preserving sentiment-bearing meaning. We compare prompting strategies, language models, and filtering configurations with the low-complexity-only DisCoCat baseline of Stein et al. At the circuit level, the strongest compression variants reduce average qubit and gate counts by more than 70 percent relative to the raw moderate-complexity subset. Across repeated training runs, GPT-4.1-mini with Prompt B achieves the highest observed mean accuracy, $0.550 \pm 0.035$, compared with $0.521 \pm 0.050$ for the baseline. Larger training splits do not necessarily improve downstream performance; across evaluated configurations, training-split size has a moderately negative association with accuracy (Pearson $r=-0.446$). These results provide exploratory evidence that LLM-assisted rewriting can make some moderate-complexity inputs usable within the evaluated DisCoCat configuration, while highlighting prompt design, filtering, and circuit-aware preprocessing as considerations for more scalable QNLP-based financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [123] [CoinRAG: Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07458)
*Gyuwan Kim,Cheoneum Park,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoinRAG是一种面向长上下文RAG的KV缓存复用方法，通过两级检索识别查询相关的细粒度语义单元（"nuggets"），并组合复用其离线计算的KV缓存来构建紧凑的上下文表示，在低prefill延迟约束下显著提升准确率和效率，相比基线平均提升5.3%的F1分数并实现新的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG优化研究虽然利用chunk级KV缓存复用来避免处理冗长检索上下文以提高效率，但粗粒度chunk中仍存在大量信息冗余和噪声，导致在低prefill延迟约束下难以同时最大化准确率。

Method: CoinRAG通过两阶段检索识别出与查询相关的语义单元（nuggets），将离线计算好的细粒度nugget KV缓存与chunk级上下文进行组合式复用，以语义相关且紧凑的方式高效构建学习到的上下文表示，避免对完整chunk进行编码。

Result: 在LongBench多跳问答任务上，CoinRAG显著降低了运营成本，在标准快速prefill延迟预算下平均相对提升5.3%的答案质量（F1），并超越了其他基线方法，实现了新的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: CoinRAG通过将粗粒度chunk缓存细化为语义粒度的nugget缓存复用，实现了在低延迟约束下同时优化效率与准确率的Pareto前沿，证明了细粒度KV缓存组装策略在长上下文RAG中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent optimization studies on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have exploited chunk-level KV cache reuse to avoid processing long retrieved contexts for higher efficiency, while significant information redundancy and noise still remain in the coarse-grained chunks. This paper optimizes the Pareto frontier under low prefill latency constraints while maximizing accuracy by proposing CoinRAG (Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG). The name metaphorically reflects our core mechanism: much like assembling small tokens (or "coins") to accumulate a larger value, CoinRAG compositionally reuses offline-computed, fine-grained nugget caches to form a learned contextual representation efficiently in a more semantically relevant but compact manner. Specifically, instead of full-chunk encoding, CoinRAG identifies query-relevant semantic units within retrieved chunks through two-stage retrieval and seamlessly assembles their sliced KV representations with a chunk-level context. Extensive evaluations on LongBench multi-hop question answering tasks demonstrate that CoinRAG significantly reduces operational costs and outperforms the other baselines with a new Pareto frontier and an average 5.3% relative improvement in answer quality (F1) under a standard fast prefill latency budget.

</details>


### [124] [CreativeInstruct: Scalably Teaching LLMs to Balance Quality, Creativity, and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07460)
*Ananya Sahu,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CreativeInstruct，一种可扩展的指令微调方法，通过让LLM学习注入[StartCreativity]标记来平衡创造力与生成质量，并引入基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标，在叙事生成和强化学习任务上均优于后训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练（post-training）虽然提升了LLM的能力，但通常会降低其输出多样性和创造力，这对明确需要创造力的任务（如故事生成）和隐式需要创造力的任务（如强化学习）产生负面影响。

Method: 1) 提出CreativeInstruct，一种可扩展的指令微调方法，教授LLM学习注入[StartCreativity]标记以偏向创造性生成，从而平衡创造性生成与后训练模型的质量。2) 提出基于图编辑距离的结构多样性指标，以捕捉纯词汇和语义指标无法捕捉的叙事层面变化。

Result: 1) 在叙事生成上，CreativeInstruct在多样性与质量上匹配或超过多模型基线和其蒸馏变体，且推理时无需多个模型。2) 人工评估中，70.3%的情况下注释者认为CreativeInstruct的生成比后训练LLM更具创造性。3) 在RL任务中，对CreativeInstruct检查点应用GRPO在AMC上提升约4%，在MATH上提升约5个百分点。

Conclusion: CreativeInstruct证明可以通过指令微调而非依赖多模型推理来恢复后训练模型丢失的创造力，同时保持质量，并且创造性模型作为RL的基座具有更强优势。

Abstract: While post-training improves the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), it generally lowers their output diversity and creativity, negatively impacting tasks that explicitly require creativity (e.g., story generation) as well as those that require it implicitly, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL). We instead propose CreativeInstruct, a scalable instruction-tuning method that teaches LLMs to balance creative, base-model-like generations with the quality of post-trained models, by learning to inject special [StartCreativity] spans that bias generation toward creativity. Furthermore, we introduce a structural diversity metric based on graph edit distance, which captures narrative level variation missed by purely lexical and semantic metrics. On narrative generation, CreativeInstruct matches or exceeds the diversity of both multi-model baselines and distilled variants of their outputs, without sacrificing quality or requiring multiple models at inference time. These results are mirrored in our human evaluation, where we find that annotators rate CreativeInstruct generations as more creative than the post-trained LLMs' generations in 70.3% of cases. We also show the benefits of creative models as a substrate for RL: GRPO applied to a CreativeInstruct checkpoint improves by ~4% on AMC and ~5% points on MATH over the same training applied to the post-trained checkpoint.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MSB-GFM，一个面向跨域多标签节点分类的多语义基础图基础模型框架，通过多语义基表示学习和语义-结构双通道架构解决现有GFM基于单标签假设导致的多标签语义纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多标签节点分类方法只能在单图域内训练和测试，缺乏跨域泛化能力；而图基础模型（GFMs）虽能跨域迁移，但基于单标签假设，将多标签节点压缩为单一向量表示，导致多语义纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签。

Method: 提出MSB-GFM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 多语义基表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合，增强表示容量；(2) 语义-结构双通道架构结合域对抗训练，实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验证明了MSB-GFM模型的有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过语义基分解表示和双通道对抗训练架构，为跨域多标签节点分类提供了新的有效解决方案，填补了GFM在多标签场景下的空白。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [126] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE是一种专为动态字节补丁设计的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构，它利用补丁熵作为路由坐标来组织稀疏计算，取代了传统byte-level LLM中均匀的密集前馈计算，在保持下游准确率的同时实现了更低的bits-per-byte。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级LLM虽然通过动态分块实现了无分词器建模，但它们对所有补丁应用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据补丁的语义和粒度变化来调整模型容量，导致计算资源利用不均。

Method: EntropyMoE将全局补丁Transformer中的密集前馈模块替换为Top-K专家层。每个动态补丁作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定负载贡献。路由器直接从补丁熵中选择专家，利用与动态补丁构建相同的粒度信号来组织稀疏计算，补丁熵和长度共同定义特征空间以规范专家专业化。

Result: 实验表明EntropyMoE在匹配的密集和稀疏基线中实现了最低的held-out bits-per-byte，同时保持了可比的downstream accuracy。

Conclusion: 补丁熵被证明是稀疏条件计算的有效路由坐标，EntropyMoE将MoE建模扩展到了超越基于分词器表示的范围。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，用于解释稀疏MoE奖励模型中专家的行为。不同于仅通过路由权重揭示专家接收哪些提示的现有方法，CoCo使用选择-拒绝响应对之间的贡献对比来联合捕捉路由和偏好判断行为，从而更忠实地刻画专家的角色。实验表明CoCo在自动和人工评估中均比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的方法产生更连贯、更忠实、更专门化的解释，同时保持有竞争力的奖励建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏MoE奖励模型的可解释性研究仅通过路由权重来表征专家，但这只能揭示专家接收哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何评判响应，因此对专家行为的刻画不完整。

Method: 提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，使用具有最大贡献对比度的选择-拒绝响应配对来表征专家角色，同时捕捉路由和偏好行为两方面信息。

Result: 在自动和人工评估中，CoCo生成的解释比基于路由、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的方法更连贯、更忠实、更专门化，同时保持有竞争力的奖励建模精度。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对MoE奖励模型解释方法的系统研究，CoCo为理解奖励模型的行为提供了更完整的视角。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [128] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID 是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的、可解释、免训练、无监督的社区检测方法，通过模拟自然系统中的相变动力学，设计为四阶段流水线（初始化、合并、细化、选择），在真实数据集上达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于目标函数的社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，而深度学习方法虽提升性能但缺乏可解释性且依赖标注数据和训练。作者希望利用LLM的强大推理能力和世界知识，实现可解释、免标注的社区检测。

Method: LUCID采用四阶段流水线：1) 局部视角社区初始化：使用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；2) 多因素社区合并：利用LLM诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区；3) 多粒度社区细化：应用LLM诱导的由粗到细规则并行降低边界噪声；4) 全局视角社区选择：基于拓扑紧密度和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为无监督方法取得了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID验证了LLM在社区检测中的潜力，通过将隐式知识转化为显式、可解释的逻辑规则，在无需训练和标注的情况下达到了超越现有方法的效果，为可解释的图分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [129] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ADIAS框架，将智能体设计从候选中心优化转变为问题中心优化，通过显式维护持久化问题状态来引导自动智能体设计，在五个交互基准上平均超越最强基线25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动智能体设计方法采用候选中心视角，跨轮次的修复经验围绕候选智能体组织，导致修复进展隐式化，造成修复目标定位低效、部分进展整合缓慢、无效干预跨轮次传播等问题。

Method: 提出问题中心智能体优化公式，并实现ADIAS框架，包含两个核心机制：(1)持久化问题状态——维护稳定的问题身份、生命周期状态、支持证据和干预-结果历史；(2)问题引导优化——利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，用于后续聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准测试中，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，并在四个骨干模型上取得一致提升。消融实验显示移除持久化问题状态或将问题中心修订替换为候选中心策略会导致高达40.7%的性能下降。

Conclusion: 问题中心视角比候选中心视角更能有效组织跨轮次的修复经验，显式持久化问题状态是提升自动智能体设计效率与效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [130] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP)，通过问题对比教师选择机制识别样本特定的中间层，并将其注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，从而在首个语言模型层之前预测视觉token重要性并完成剪枝，无需注意力图即可实现高效视觉token剪枝，在LLaVA-NeXT-7B上仅保留5.56%的视觉token即保持97.5%性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)虽性能强大，但处理大量视觉token的成本高昂。现有视觉token剪枝方法依赖固定中间层的注意力引导，存在两个问题：(1)最能响应问题的注意力层因样本而异，固定层次选择次优；(2)获取中间层注意力需要先让视觉token通过多个语言模型层，此时大量计算已消耗，剪枝收益有限。

Method: 提出MAP框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Question Contrastive Teacher Selection：通过对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力差异，为每个样本动态识别特定的教师层；(2) 将选定的教师层注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征直接估计视觉token重要性。推理时，MAP结合预测的重要性分数与多样性准则，在首个语言模型层之前完成token剪枝，无需注意力图且兼容现有推理加速技术。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B模型的十个基准测试上，MAP仅使用5.56%的视觉token即可保留未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测蒸馏，有效解决了固定中间层剪枝的次优性和计算浪费问题，实现了高效且与现有加速技术兼容的视觉token剪枝方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [131] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WebGrader，一种自进化的程序化评分器，用于大规模语言模型生成网站的任务。它通过自动从网站请求中推导交互流程（Flow Contract），在实时浏览器中执行并以执行结果作为强化学习奖励，解决了奖励设计瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练网站生成模型受限于奖励设计：手写浏览器脚本成本高昂且难以覆盖开放式需求，而VLM和GUI代理评分器可扩展但可能在关键状态出现前就给出判定，导致评分不准确。

Method: WebGrader将测试规划、动作落地、证据收集和语义判断分离。从每个网站请求自动推导交互流程并表示为可执行的Flow Contract；在实时浏览器中落地生成的项目，将目标动作与源代码和实时DOM对齐；沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久化状态证据；通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，并在策略训练前冻结提升的技能图。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练的8B策略达到52.01%功能成功率，比匹配的外观加脚本奖励高出7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953满分，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: 通过仅在观察到请求的转换后才发出Pass判定，WebGrader解决了VLM和GUI代理评分器提前判定导致的不准确问题，实现了更可靠的功能验证和更强的策略训练效果。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [132] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4，一个认知启发的跨概念创造力评估框架，基于中文成语构建，包含编码（项目构建）和解码（模型推理）两大组件，并推出C4-Eval评测集（184个合成项+37个人类创作项，884个主答案恢复用例）。在10个MLLM上测试显示，最强的闭源模型主准确率仅约50%，开源模型更低，揭示当前多模态大模型在通过跨概念关系解码创意编码意义方面存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造性能力在设计、沟通、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但相比于准确性导向的任务，创造性任务缺乏明确目标和奖励信号，难以评估。跨概念理解是接受性创造力的核心认知能力，但目前缺乏系统化的评测方法。

Method: 1) 将项目构建操作化为跨概念编码，将模型推理操作化为跨概念解码；2) 构建C4框架，编码组件通过人工标注和第三方评审的跨概念网络中的桥梁路径将目标槽映射到可成像替代概念，支持带明确结构的批量生成（难度由桥梁数量和深度索引，且有精确答案）；3) 实例化C4-Eval评测集，含184个合成项和37个人类创作项；4) 每个项目在五种任务设置下实例化，共生成884个主答案恢复用例。

Result: 在10个评估的MLLM中，最强的闭源模型主准确率达到50.7%和48.0%，开源模型显著较低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥梁提示和解释请求仅带来适度提升。结果暴露了当前MLLM在通过跨概念关系解码创意编码意义方面的实质性差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在接受性创造力（特别是跨概念理解）方面仍有显著不足，C4框架提供了一种可扩展、有明确结构和精确答案的评测方法。候选约束的显著效果表明模型在受限条件下表现更好，但创造性解码能力仍是大模型的重要瓶颈。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [133] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan是一个两阶段的学位规划系统，严格分离"课程目录提取"和"个人学位路径优化"两个过程。CatalogBrowse阶段在无用户信息的情况下从异构大学来源（目录、PDF、JSON端点）提取结构化的三份JSON文档，通过置信度加权的期望边际收益打分、确定性解析器加AST回退机制以及闭包证书来保证完整性；DegreeMap阶段仅消费这些文档，将其编译为类型化需求超图，用CP-SAT进行字典序优化。在100所大学的广泛测试和6所学校的密集测试中，CatalogBrowse达到96.2%的库存召回率和88.7%的掩码源恢复率（节省47%的源访问量），DegreeMap保持100%的硬可行性并提升个性化效用+0.066，完整流水线认证99.5%的请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从官方大学来源规划学位需要先解决两个顺序问题：首先从无共享模式的异构来源（目录、院系页面、JSON端点、PDF）重建课程体系，然后才能在先修逻辑和重叠要求约束下优化学生个性化路径。将两个问题耦合会使各自失败模式相互掩盖，因为驱动自身爬取的规划器永远不会学到当前计划不需要的事实。因此需要强制"先提取后规划"的边界。

Method: KnowPlan采用严格的两阶段架构。第一阶段CatalogBrowse在无用户画像的情况下探索，通过低置信度期望边际收益/单位源访问来评分合法操作，使用平台适配器进行确定性解析，并以跨度约束的子句到AST模型作为回退，最终以索引、模式、溯源和引用完整性的闭包证书终止。输出为三份带溯源链接的JSON文档。第二阶段DegreeMap仅消费这些文档，将其编译为类型化需求超图，用CP-SAT在硬可行性、完成时间跨度、负荷与风险、个性化效用和期权价值上进行字典序优化，使每个阶段在前一阶段的已证最优内部优化，并在求解器预算内保持可认证性。

Result: 在100所大学的广泛跟踪和6所学校的密集跟踪中：CatalogBrowse达到96.2%的库存召回率和88.7%的掩码源恢复率，比穷举爬虫节省47%的源访问量；DegreeMap在保持100%硬可行性的同时，将个性化效用比最强基线提升+0.066；完整流水线认证99.5%的请求，与特权黄金图的效用差距仅为0.015。

Conclusion: KnowPlan证明了在学位规划中强制"提取优先"的架构边界是有效的——通过隔离两个阶段的失败模式并量化阶段间接口而非假设其正确性，系统在数据召回、个性化效用和端到端可认证性上都达到了高水平，同时大幅减少了不必要的源访问。

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget. Across a 100-university broad track and a six-school dense track, CatalogBrowse reaches 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than an exhaustive crawler, DegreeMap holds 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap to the privileged gold graph of 0.015.

</details>


### [134] [TaskSense: Focusing on What Matters in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06544)
*SM Mazharul Islam,Manfred Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TaskSense提出了一种任务中心的世界建模框架，通过可微分的随机空间注意力机制和辅助逆动力学目标，在潜在编码前强制聚焦任务相关区域，仅重建被关注区域而非完整观测，从而在视觉干扰环境下显著提升模型鲁棒性，同时保持与DreamerV3相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型（如DreamerV3）通过重建完整观测来学习潜在状态，但任务相关内容通常只占观测的一小部分，背景杂波和干扰物消耗了大量表征容量。重建与控制的优化目标不匹配，导致潜在表征偏向建模任务无关的视觉内容，稀释了控制相关特征的学习信号，严重损害了视觉干扰下的下游性能。

Method: TaskSense包含三个关键设计：(1) 在潜在编码之前引入可微分的随机空间注意力机制，该机制以先前潜在状态为条件；(2) 通过辅助逆动力学目标引导注意力聚焦控制相关区域；(3) 世界模型仅重建被关注的区域而非完整观测，且解码器以采样得到的注意力图为条件，以保证随机注意力下重建的一致性。

Result: 在DeepMind Control Suite上与DreamerV3基线性能相当；在Distracting Control Suite上持续优于DreamerV3，大幅提升了对视觉干扰的鲁棒性。定性分析确认，由逆动力学监督引导的注意力能够一致地定位控制相关区域，同时抑制无关视觉内容。

Conclusion: TaskSense验证了"任务相关性先于重建"这一核心思想的有效性：通过在编码前施加任务导向的注意力机制并结合逆动力学监督，世界模型可以更高效地利用表征容量，在不牺牲标准环境性能的前提下显著增强对视觉干扰的鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models for visual control typically learn compact latent states by reconstructing observations, implicitly encouraging representations to preserve information across the entire visual input. However, task-relevant content often occupies only a small fraction of the observation, while background clutter and distractors consume valuable representational capacity. This mismatch between visual reconstruction and control objectives biases latent representations to model task-irrelevant visual content, diluting learning signals for control-relevant features and severely degrading downstream performance under visual distractions. We introduce TaskSense, a task-centric world modeling framework that enforces task relevance before latent encoding through a differentiable stochastic spatial attention mechanism conditioned on the previous latent state. To steer attention toward control-relevant regions, we augment training with an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective. Rather than reconstructing the full observation, the world model reconstructs only the attended regions, encouraging latent representations to preserve task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant visual content. The decoder is further conditioned on the sampled attention map, enabling consistent reconstruction despite stochastic attention. Compared with the DreamerV3 baseline, TaskSense maintains competitive performance on the DeepMind Control Suite while consistently outperforming DreamerV3 on the Distracting Control Suite, demonstrating substantially improved robustness to visual distractions. Qualitative analysis further confirms that the learned attention, guided by inverse-dynamics supervision, consistently localizes control-relevant regions while suppressing irrelevant visual content.

</details>


### [135] [Divergent Response Modes in Frontier Language Models Under Steering Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06578)
*Ali Jalal-Kamali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了六个前沿语言模型在显式引导压力下的行为可引导性，使用300对引导条目和24,480条判断，发现模型不仅在引导程度上有差异，在响应模式类型上也存在本质不同。其中GPT-5会拒绝透露推理过程但保留答案，Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗抑制指令。通过线性探针以0.87的准确率从Llama模型残差流解码行为，并能在干预扫描中将行为从0%驱动至86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对前沿模型在训练数据、目标和安全管线上的差异是否会导致可测量的行为差异探索不足，特别是在显式引导压力下模型的可引导性差异尚未被系统研究。

Method: 使用300个配对的基础/引导条目（覆盖价值观冲突、推理激发和推理抑制三类，外加40个验证条目），六个模型互为盲审评判者，基于固定行为准则对每条响应分类，得到24,480条判断，通过留一法共识评分。此外对Llama模型进行线性探针分析残差流，并通过干预扫描验证行为方向的可操控性。

Result: 模型不仅在引导改变行为的程度上不同，在响应模式类型上也有差异，某些响应模式仅出现在一两个模型中。GPT-5拒绝披露推理但保留答案（99%对比其他模型0%）；Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5以不同方式抵抗显式抑制指令。线性探针在0.87留出准确率下解码行为，注入该方向可将行为从0%驱动至86%。所有发现在分配预算修复和假设盲评判对照实验中均成立。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的差异化训练会产生本质不同的行为模式，且可引导性差异是可测量、可量化的。这些差异可追溯到模型内部表征，为理解和调控模型行为提供了可操作的干预手段。

Abstract: Frontier language models are trained using distinct data, objectives, and safety pipelines. Whether these differences produce measurably different behaviors under explicit steering pressure remains underexplored. This study evaluates behavioral steerability across six frontier models from six developers using 300 paired base and steered items over three categories: values-conflict, reasoning-elicitation, and reasoning-suppression (plus 40 validation items). All six models act as blind peer judges and classify every response based on fixed behavioral rubrics. The resulting 24,480 judgments are scored by leave-one-out consensus. We find that models differ not just in how much steering shifts their behavior but in what kind (mode) of response they give, and some response modes appear in only one or two of them. GPT-5 deflects requests to disclose its reasoning while leaving its answer intact (99% vs. 0% for all other models). Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5 resist explicit suppression instructions and in different ways. Using Llama as the open-weight model, we trace the largest behavioral split to its internals. A linear probe decodes the behavior from the residual stream at 0.87 held-out accuracy while injecting that direction during generation drives the behavior from 0% to 86% across an intervention sweep. Every finding holds under both a token-budget remediation and a control experiment with a hypothesis-blind judgment prompt.

</details>


### [136] [Automated item evaluation: Predicting item acceptance and rejection using LLM-generated critiques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06609)
*Hotaka Maeda,Yikai Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于52,759道标准化测试题目（英语语言艺术和数学）的历史拒收数据，利用DeBERTaV3-large分类器对原始题目文本进行微调，并融合Qwen3生成的题目批判表示，构建近全面的自动化题目评估（AIE）模型。融合模型达到最优性能（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80），数学预测显著优于英语，降低决策阈值可在自动化题目生成场景中提高敏感性，但在偏见/公平性相关题目识别上仍有局限，凸显人类审查的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统题目评估依赖人工专家审查和现场测试，成本高昂且耗时。研究旨在构建免于人工审查和现场测试的自动化题目评估模型，利用大规模历史拒收数据预测题目是否会被拒绝，从而降低题目开发与评估的负担。

Method: 使用52,759道题目（34%被永久拒收）作为训练数据，构建三种模型：（1）基于原始题目文本微调的DeBERTaV3-large分类器；（2）基于Qwen3生成题目批判的DeBERTa分类器；（3）融合两者表示的融合模型。评估指标包括准确率、F1、AUC、敏感性和特异性，并对不同拒收原因和决策阈值进行分析。

Result: 融合模型整体性能最佳（Accuracy=.75, F1=.64, AUC=.80, Sensitivity=.64, Specificity=.81）。数学题目预测（F1=.73, AUC=.86）显著优于英语（F1=.51, AUC=.72）。将决策阈值从.5降至.25可将平均敏感性提升至英语.88和数学.91，但特异性降至.31和.56。融合题目批判可改善大多数拒收原因的预测，但模型难以识别涉及偏见、敏感性、公平性或可及性的题目，且更难的题目被赋予更高的拒收概率。

Conclusion: 基于文本的自动化题目评估在部分领域具有可行性，可减少人工审查和现场测试负担，尤其在自动化题目生成场景下降低阈值可发挥更大实用价值。然而对于涉及公平性问题的题目，模型表现不佳，必须保留人类审查环节以确保题目评估的公平性与质量。

Abstract: Automated item evaluation (AIE) refers to the use of computational methods to assess item quality without requiring manual expert review or field testing of the items under evaluation. We aimed to build a near-comprehensive AIE model by predicting item acceptance and rejection from item text using historical rejection data from a large-scale standardized testing program. The dataset contained 52,759 English language arts (ELA) and mathematics items with 34% permanently rejected from future operational use. Rejection reasons included poor psychometric properties, content issues, bias and sensitivity concerns, and non-content issues. We fine-tuned a DeBERTaV3-large classifier on raw item text, a second DeBERTa classifier on Qwen3-generated item critiques, and a fusion model combining representations from both. The fusion model achieved the strongest overall performance (Accuracy = .75, F1 = .64, AUC = .80, Sensitivity = .64, Specificity = .81). Prediction for math (F1 = .73, AUC = .86) was considerably more accurate than ELA (F1 = .51, AUC = .72). Lowering the decision threshold from .5 to .25 raised average sensitivity for ELA and math to .88 and .91, while reducing specificity to .31 and .56, respectively, which may be preferable in automated item generation contexts where generating items is cheaper than evaluating them. Incorporating item critiques alongside raw item text improved performance across most rejection reasons. The model assigned higher rejection probabilities to more difficult items. However, the fusion model struggled to identify items flagged for bias, sensitivity, fairness, or accessibility, especially for ELA. These findings suggest that text-based AIE is feasible in some areas and may offer a practical tool for reducing the burden of manual review and field testing, while also underscoring the importance of human review for items with fairness concerns.

</details>


### [137] [NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621)
*Bin Wang,Yan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NxN E-valuation算法，一种基于e-value的假设认证方法，无需构建专门的认证流程（如专用零假设），只需足够大的数据集即可验证假设。特别适用于LLM探索系统，利用自然存在的大规模训练集，让不同样本互为零假设，直接实现条件随机化检验（CRT）来认证每个假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在提出假设方面表现出色但存在严重的幻觉问题，导致无法直接采信LLM的输出。现有解决方案各有缺陷：LLM自我验证存在循环验证问题，持出测试可能因虚假相关性而让错误假设蒙混过关。因此需要一种无需特定案例认证程序即可验证假设的通用方法。

Method: 提出NxN E-valuation算法，利用天然存在的大规模训练集，让不同的样本彼此充当零假设（互为null hypothesis），从而直接实现条件随机化检验（CRT）来认证每个假设。该方法基于e-value理论，无需构建每个案例特定的认证程序。

Result: 该方法可以作为LLM循环验证和持出数据测试的通用更优替代方案，前提是LLM生成的是适用于每个个体样本的假设。

Conclusion: NxN E-valuation提供了一种方便、通用的假设认证方案，特别适合LLM驱动的探索系统，通过样本间互为零假设的设计，有效缓解LLM幻觉问题。

Abstract: We propose NxN E-valuation, a handy, e-value-based hypothesis-certification algorithm that lets a hypothesis be verified without building any case-specific certification procedure---such as constructing a dedicated null hypothesis---as long as a large enough dataset is available. The method is especially suited to LLM-based exploration systems, where LLMs are remarkably good at proposing hypotheses but suffer badly from hallucination; this hallucination prevents us from harvesting LLM outputs directly, and existing remedies each fall short. The most common solutions include letting the LLM verify or correct itself circular verification and held-out testing (where false hypotheses can still pass via spurious correlations), among other remedies detailed in the introduction. To resolve this, NxN E-valuation exploits the naturally existing large training set and lets different samples serve as null hypotheses for one another. This design directly realizes a conditional randomization test (CRT) that certifies each hypothesis. The approach can be a universally better replacement for at least LLM circular verification and held-out-data testing, provided the LLM's generations are hypotheses that apply to each individual sample.

</details>


### [138] [Shape Your Feed: An LLM-based Agentic System for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06632)
*Ziyun Xu,Bosen Ding,Yue Zhang,Ji Qi,Qingyuan Song,Jizhou Huang,Liwei Wang,Jefferey Santelli,Yue Weng,Qichao Que,Zhenheng Yang,Junfeng Pan,Linhong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Shape Your Feed (SYF)，一个基于LLM的智能体推荐框架，实现实时、多模态的内容协同策展，解决了工业推荐系统被动排序范式与用户显式表达兴趣之间日益扩大的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统采用被动排序范式，仅从隐式行为信号（点击、停留时间）推断用户偏好，而非显式的自然语言输入，导致用户显式兴趣与算法推送内容之间存在持续偏差，用户无法实时表达细腻偏好或引导信息流方向。

Method: SYF采用三层架构：(i) Perception Flow——从文本提示、语音命令和UI交互中捕获细粒度用户意图；(ii) Serving Flow——基于持久化语义画像进行实时智能体重排序和候选剪辑；(iii) Self-Evolution Flow——通过DPO和LLM-as-a-Judge集成使系统行为与人类判断对齐。

Result: 离线评估中，SYF的对齐评分模块达到98.85%准确率，显著优于强少样本基线；大规模在线A/B实验表明SYF提升了信息流相关性和用户情感。

Conclusion: SYF展示了在工业环境中实现交互式、用户可控推荐的实用且可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems predominantly adopt a passive ranking paradigm that infers user preferences from implicit behavioral signals (e.g., clicks, dwell time) rather than explicit, natural language inputs. As a result, users experience a persistent discrepancy between their explicit interests and what passive behavioral algorithms deliver, limiting their ability to express nuanced preferences or steer their feed in real time. To address this growing gap between how recommendations are optimized and how users wish to articulate their interests, we present Shape Your Feed (SYF), an LLM-based agentic recommendation framework that enables real-time, multimodal co-curation of content. SYF employs a three-tier architecture: (i) a Perception Flow that captures fine-grained user intent from text prompts, voice commands, and UI interactions; (ii) a Serving Flow that performs real-time agentic re-ranking and pruning of candidate items, grounded in a persistent Semantic Profile encoding evolving user preferences; and (iii) a Self-Evolution Flow that aligns system behavior with human judgments via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and an LLM-as-a-Judge ensemble. Offline evaluations show that SYF's alignment scoring module achieves 98.85% accuracy, substantially improving over strong few-shot baselines. Large-scale online A/B experiments on production traffic further demonstrate that SYF improves feed relevance and user sentiment, indicating a practical and scalable path toward interactive, user-steerable recommendation in industrial settings.

</details>


### [139] [TRACE: A Multi-Layer Benchmark for Human AI Controller Coordination Under Drift and Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06657)
*Joshua Zuniga,Srinivasan Subramanian,Ramya Madhuri Narapureddy,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个多层级漂移检测基准（基于ALFRED的1,918条漂移轨迹），覆盖状态、观察、决策、规则、控制五个执行层，并配套去泄漏协议与基线研究，发现简单模型在该符号基准上不逊于重型注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络物理与AI辅助系统将人类操作员、AI决策模块和自动控制器耦合在同一控制回路中，可信赖性取决于整个回路而非单一模型，但缺乏时间对齐的多层轨迹基准来诊断漂移和故障如何在各层间传播。

Method: 从ALFRED基准注入受控漂移构建1,918条漂移轨迹，每条轨迹包含五个执行层（状态、观察、决策、规则、控制）的时间对齐逐步骤记录，标注漂移类型、受影响层、起始时间、责任主体和因果机制，经独立评分者验证；配套去泄漏协议并开展经典、循环和注意力三类模型族的基线研究。

Result: 在诚实协议下，各模型族的漂移识别与归因均显著高于随机和多数类基线：受影响层macro-F1约0.70，责任主体约0.85，因果机制约0.49；重型注意力模型在该符号基准上无优势。

Conclusion: 该基准填补了多层协调系统漂移诊断的空白，证明了漂移可识别、可归因，且揭示了模型复杂度与该任务性能不相关的发现。

Abstract: Modern cyber-physical and AI-assisted systems couple human operators, AI decision modules, and automated controllers in a single control loop, so trustworthiness depends on the whole loop, not any one model. Yet no standard benchmark captures time-aligned, multi-layer traces of how drift and failures propagate across these layers, so we cannot diagnose where coordination breaks down, why, or how to recover. This paper targets one facet of that gap: drift, a deviation that can originate in any stack layer and that conventional single-modality monitoring cannot localize to a layer or pin to an onset time. We construct a benchmark by injecting controlled drift into traces derived from ALFRED, a grounded-instruction benchmark for everyday household tasks, yielding 1,918 drifted traces. Each trace is a time-aligned sequence of per-step records across five execution layers (state, observation, decision, rules, control), labeled with the drift type, affected layer, onset time, responsible actor, and causal mechanism, and validated by independent raters with inter-annotator agreement reported. We pair the dataset with a leak-aware protocol that removes a near-perfect onset leak, and a baseline study across classical, recurrent, and attention-based model families. Under this honest protocol, drift is identifiable and attributable well above random and majority baselines across every family (affected layer macro-F1 near 0.70, responsible actor near 0.85, causal mechanism near 0.49), and heavy attention offers no advantage over simpler models on this symbolic benchmark.

</details>


### [140] [CellWorld: From Gene-Level Reconstruction to Latent Cell Prediction in Spatial Transcriptomics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06659)
*Haiping Liu,Qian Zhao,Lijing Lin,Jingyuan Sun,Hongpeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CellWorld，一种用于空间转录组的潜在空间预测预训练基础模型。与现有方法直接重建掩码基因不同，CellWorld预测掩码细胞的潜在表征，在4600万个人类细胞语料库上预训练四个规模变体（5.74M到94.56M参数），在4个外部数据集上超越所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组基础模型主要重建掩码基因身份或表达值，这会鼓励模型复制检测特异的技术变异，限制表征的可迁移性。作者希望将预测目标从观测基因测量转移到潜在细胞表征，避免直接重建技术变异，从而提升表征的泛化能力。

Method: CellWorld从可见空间上下文和有限的局部表达提示中预测掩码细胞的潜在表征，而非直接预测基因测量值。作者预训练了四个参数规模从5.74M到94.56M的变体，在4600万人类细胞上进行规模可控实验，并系统研究模型容量、优化程度和生物来源多样性对性能的影响。

Result: 控制规模实验表明性能随模型容量提升而改善（尤其在空间任务上），空间迁移更依赖于充分优化和广泛的生物来源多样性而非单纯细胞数量。在4个外部数据集上，即使最小的CellWorld-Small（5.74M参数）也在全部11个线性探针基准和7个微调空间基准上超越所有基线。仅用5%语料库预训练的冻结CellWorld-Large在所有7个空间基准上超越所有完全微调基线。

Conclusion: 潜在空间预测预训练为空间转录组基础模型提供了可扩展的路径，通过避免直接重建技术变异实现了更强的表征可迁移性，且较小的模型也能在空间任务上展现优异性能。

Abstract: This paper shows that latent-space predictive pretraining can provide a scalable route to foundation models for spatial transcriptomics. Existing spatial transcriptomics foundation models primarily reconstruct masked gene identities or expression values, potentially encouraging the reproduction of assay-specific technical variation and limiting representation transferability. To avoid directly reconstructing such variation, we shift the prediction target from observed gene measurements to latent cell representations and introduce CellWorld, which predicts the latent representations of masked cells from visible spatial context and a limited partial-expression hint. We pretrain four CellWorld variants, spanning 5.74M to 94.56M trainable parameters, on a corpus of 46 million human cells. Our controlled scaling experiments show that performance improves with model capacity, particularly on spatial tasks, while spatial transfer depends more on sufficient optimization and broad biological source diversity than on cell count alone. Across four held-out datasets, even CellWorld-Small, with 5.74M trainable parameters, outperforms every baseline on all 11 linear-probe benchmarks and all seven fine-tuned spatial benchmarks. Most notably, a frozen CellWorld-Large pretrained on only 5\% of the corpus with broad biological source coverage outperforms every fully fine-tuned baseline across all seven spatial benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/UoM-HealthAI/CellWorld.

</details>


### [141] [Vehicle routing problem using deep reinforcement learning - A case study about truck planning in the industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06668)
*Siliang Lu,Dan Hu,Lili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个工业物流案例，展示了基于深度强化学习（DRL）的车辆路径优化在实际外部卡车网络设计中的应用，相比基线方案可实现超过10%的成本节约，并展望了DRL算法在未来更多VRP变体中的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（VRP）作为运输领域持续存在的核心挑战，尽管已有大量优化模型和算法（从经典的TSP到更一般的VRP），但实际工业场景中往往面临多重约束、信息不透明、不确定性以及非理性人为行为等复杂因素，导致数学模型难以在保持最优结果的同时直接部署。因此，需要探索能够在现实场景中有效应用的解决方案。

Method: 本文针对三个不同的物流用例（涉及外部卡车网络设计），采用基于深度强化学习（DRL）的车辆路径优化方法，通过工业案例研究的方式，将DRL算法部署到实际物流场景中并进行路径优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，经强化学习智能体优化的路径相比基线结果可实现超过10%的总成本降低，验证了DRL方法在实际工业物流场景中的有效性和经济性优势。

Conclusion: 本文通过实际工业案例验证了深度强化学习在车辆路径优化中的实用价值，并指出未来研究方向是将DRL算法推广到更多VRP变体，以应对更广泛的物流应用需求。

Abstract: As an important component of the supply chain industry, transportation has experienced rapid development in the past decade with the assistance of digital platforms and intelligent algorithms. Within the field of transportation research, Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP) has remained a persistent and enduring challenge. In the realm of management science, experts, and scholars from both the industrial and academic sectors have continuously explored optimization models and algorithms to effectively address routing problems, from the classical Traveling Salesman Problem to the more general Vehicle Routing Problem. These models and algorithms are applied in real-world industrial scenarios to achieve cost optimization and reduce carbon footprints. However, due to the complexity of real-world problems, numerous specific constraints are often added, and challenges such as information opacity, uncertainty, and irrational human behavior may arise. Therefore, deploying and optimizing mathematical models for VRP in practical scenarios while maintaining optimal results poses numerous challenges. This paper discusses and provides solutions for three different logistic use cases involving external truck network design. Through these industrial case study, the paper introduces how deep reinforcement learning-based vehicle routing optimization has been implemented. As a result, it can be observed that the routes optimized by reinforcement learning agent have over 10% total cost compared to baseline results. Furthermore, the paper proposes that in future research, DRL algorithms for vehicle routing problems could be generalized into more variations of VRP.

</details>


### [142] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Coarse-Grained Molecular Dynamics of Polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06694)
*Joohee Choi,Junhyeong Lee,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CGMas，一个多智能体框架，通过自然语言描述聚合物和目标分辨率，全自动化完成粗粒化建模流程，包括拓扑构建、平衡、映射、势能推导和验证，在27个均聚物和共聚物任务中全部成功，密度误差5%内22个，并将模拟时间从38-88分钟降至1分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自下而上的粗粒化建模繁琐耗时，因为CG分辨率是设计选择，可转移的参数集通常不可用，每个聚合物映射都需要重新推导势能函数，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 构建CGMas多智能体框架：1) 大型语言模型推理智能体根据聚合物名称推断AA拓扑结构；2) 分层自校正机制解决不饱和、含杂原子和极性聚合物的物理错误；3) 下游智能体负责系统平衡、CG映射、通过Boltzmann反演推导势能、以及针对原子参考的基准验证。

Result: CGMas完成了全部27个均聚物和共聚物任务，22个任务的AA密度匹配在5%以内，并将模拟时间从38-88分钟缩短至1分钟。

Conclusion: 智能体化LLM可以作为自动化聚合物粗粒化的可行途径，显著提高建模效率和可及性。

Abstract: Coarse-grained (CG) molecular dynamics extends polymer simulation beyond the scales accessible to all-atom (AA) methods, but bottom-up CG modeling is laborious. The CG resolution is a design choice, so a transferable parameter set is generally not available and the potentials are derived anew for each polymer mapping. Here we present CGMas, a multi-agent framework that automates topology construction, equilibration, mapping, potential derivation, and validation from a natural-language specification of the polymer and target resolution. A large-language-model (LLM) reasoning agent infers the AA topology from polymer name, while layered self-correction resolves physical errors common to unsaturated, heteroatom-containing, and polar polymers. Downstream agents equilibrate the system, map it onto CG representation, derive potentials through Boltzmann inversion, and benchmark the model against its atomistic reference. CGMas completed all 27 homopolymer and copolymer tasks, matched the AA density to within 5% in 22, and reduced simulation from 38-88 min to 1 min, establishing agentic LLMs as a route to automated polymer coarse-graining.

</details>


### [143] [AgentPatch: Coarse-to-Fine Weak-Task Repair for Merging Agentic Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06699)
*Zibo Shao,Baochen Xiong,Chengdong Xu,Linhui Xiao,Kaichen Li,Haoran Gong,Yan Li,Yaguang Song,Xiaoshan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentPatch，一种无需训练的粗到细修复框架，用于解决智能体多模态大语言模型（MLLM）合并中面临的不对称能力保留和行为关键遗忘两大挑战，通过恢复弱任务独特信号和行为关键补丁，生成单一静态检查点，在六个基准上显著改善合并模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体MLLM模型针对特定工具或环境高度特化，难以合并为统一通用模型。模型合并过程中存在两大挑战：一是不同交互复杂度的能力保留不均匀，导致弱任务退化；二是关键决策行为丢失，使长程执行任务脱轨。

Method: 提出AgentPatch框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 选择稳定的合并骨干；(2) 通过弱任务独特残差恢复（Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery）修复被稀释的弱任务信号；(3) 应用智能体引导的行为关键补丁（Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch），在明确能力保护下恢复关键决策行为。整个过程无需训练，不依赖路由或集成，最终生成单一静态检查点。

Result: 在六个智能体和多模态基准上的实验表明，AgentPatch能改善多种合并骨干的性能，缓解弱任务退化问题，并在弱任务恢复与保留互补搜索及智能体视觉处理能力之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: AgentPatch有效解决了智能体MLLM合并中的关键挑战，为构建通用智能体模型提供了一种无需训练的实用修复方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) extend multimodal perception and reasoning with planning, tool use, and interaction in dynamic environments. Yet current models are specialized for particular tools or environments, complicating consolidation into a single generalist. We formulate Agentic MLLM Merging and identify two challenges: asymmetric capability preservation, whereby capabilities with different interaction complexity are retained unevenly, producing weak tasks after merging, and behavior-critical forgetting, whereby losing decisive actions can derail long-horizon execution. We propose AgentPatch, a training-free coarse-to-fine repair framework. It selects a stable merged backbone, restores diluted weak-task-specific signals through Weak-Task Unique Residual Recovery, and applies an Agent-Guided Behavior-Critical Patch that recovers decisive behaviors under explicit capability protection. AgentPatch produces a single static checkpoint without routing or ensembles. Experiments across six agentic and multimodal benchmarks show that AgentPatch improves diverse merged backbones, alleviates weak-task degradation, and better balances weak-task recovery with the preservation of complementary search and agentic visual processing capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/ziboshao/AgentPatch.

</details>


### [144] [WebRider: Persona-Conditioned Intent Controllers for Live-Web Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06704)
*Zhi Li,Tao Zhou,Yeqing Li,Eugene Ie,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有实时网页代理仅以最终答案评估的缺陷，提出WebRider框架，将委派策略形式化为"意图契约"，通过分层架构（控制器-中间层-工具层）实现可审计、可评判、可学习的网页任务执行，并发布包含4,096个实时网页契约的RiderBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时网页代理仅评估最终答案，忽略了委派任务中策略约束（验证什么、如何处理不确定性、偏好、何时停止）的重要性。审计表明强大的控制器完成99.2%的任务，但仅38.8%的案例遵守所有策略约束，说明"完成≠忠实履行"。这暴露了当前评估体系的关键缺口。

Method: WebRider将委派策略形式化为"意图契约"——记录目标、约束、证据义务、答案形式和任务本地人格控制的运行记录。采用三层架构：顶层控制器维护契约，中间层将意图实现为受保护的（guarded）可执行动作，工具层通过浏览器、搜索和地图工具执行动作。中间受保护接口同时作为高质量训练信号，训练8B动作策略模型。

Result: 发布RiderBench基准，在42个公共网站的4,096个实时网页契约上评估，同时审计内部契约状态和可见用户体验。通过中间受保护接口训练的8B动作策略模型在固定控制器下优于仅可执行（executable-only）基线。在线审计显示当前系统存在策略遵守率远低于完成率的严重差距（38.8% vs 99.2%）。

Conclusion: 通过将浏览路径作为一等公民，WebRider实现了可审计、可人工评判、可学习的系统，不将动作实现与最终答案决策混为一谈。该工作为网页代理评估提供了新的范式，使策略忠实性成为核心评估维度。

Abstract: Delegating a web task involves more than asking a question; it requires transferring a policy: what to verify, how to handle uncertainty, which preferences matter, and when to stop. Yet, current live-web agents are evaluated solely on the final answer, ignoring the policy constraints that define the delegation. A plausible final answer can conceal violations of that policy. Our full live audit reveals this critical gap: a strong controller completes 99.2% of tasks but honors all policy constraints in only 38.8% of cases. Finishing does not imply fidelity. WebRider bridges this gap by formalizing the delegated policy as an intent contract---an operational record of goals, constraints, evidence obligations, answer form, and task-local persona controls that must hold even as web pages change. WebRider employs a hierarchical architecture: a top-layer controller maintains the contract, a middle layer realizes intentions as guarded executable actions, and a tool layer executes these actions via browser, search, and maps tools. Our benchmark, RiderBench, evaluates this design on 4,096 live-web contracts across 42 public websites, auditing both the internal contract state and the visible user experience to determine if a rollout preserved its policy and if the steps were persona-consistent. The guarded middle interface also serves as a high-quality training signal; an 8B action-policy model trained through this interface outperforms executable-only baselines under a fixed controller. By making the browsing path a first-class object, WebRider enables a system that is auditable, human-judgeable, and learnable without conflating action realization with final-answer decisions. Dataset URL: hf.co/datasets/WebRider/WebRider.

</details>


### [145] [MolBioKG: Grounding Out-of-Graph Molecules in Biomedical Knowledge Graphs via Multi-Resolution Structural Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06713)
*Yiming Zhang,Hikaru Shindo,Shuan Chen,Kaushalya Madhawa,Jun Jin Choong,Yuna Oikawa,Takashi Fujiwara,Keisuke Ozawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MolBioKG，一个双层系统，通过多分辨率结构锚定将未注册（图外）分子连接到生物医学知识图谱中，解决冷启动问题。系统连接274万分子索引与960万边KG，仅需SMILES字符串即可检索结构相关实体并遍历其生物医学邻域，无需任务特定训练。包含静态多锚定检索（RFF）和Adapt-KG（基于LLM的工具使用策略）两种推理机制，在多项任务中优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准生物医学KG管道假设查询分子已存在于图实体中，导致未注册分子与KG脱节。这种冷启动问题（out-of-graph molecule problem）严重限制了KG在新药发现场景中的应用，因为大量候选分子尚未被收录到现有知识图谱中。
